- **sdd-db-context**: Phase 0 du reverse base de données — construit le Database Context (SSoT versionné) avant toute remontée en User Story. Récolte déterministe des faits (tables, colonnes, PK/FK, index, contraintes CHECK, corps d'objets, matrice CRUD, graphe de dépendances, plan de vagues), puis interprétation par l'agent reverse-db-architect (glossaire, sous-domaines, rôles, risques, questions ouvertes). Produit db-context.json + un arbre Markdown découpé par objet, réutilisé tant que la base n'a pas changé. Ne modifie JAMAIS la base. ([zekiriabd/SDD-Pro](https://github.com/zekiriabd/SDD-Pro/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sdd-db-context.md) · ★196 · 2 copies)
- **sdd-db-reverse**: Reverse engineering d'UN objet SQL exécutable — procédure stockée, fonction, vue ou trigger (P0.1 2026-07-24) — en lecture seule via la connection string de stack.md. Introspecte l'objet, génère sa User Story (1 objet SQL = 1 US) et met à jour/crée la FEAT du module métier auquel il appartient. Multi-dialecte — SQL Server (live-validé), PostgreSQL + Oracle + MySQL/MariaDB (scaffold-validés, runtime live pending — downgrade PostgreSQL audit 2026-08-29). Ne modifie JAMAIS la base. ([zekiriabd/SDD-Pro](https://github.com/zekiriabd/SDD-Pro/blob/HEAD/.sdd/commands/sdd-db-reverse.md) · ★196 · 2 copies)
- **sdd-db-reverse-full**: Reverse engineering de TOUS les objets SQL exécutables d'une base (lecture seule) — procédures stockées, fonctions, vues et triggers (P0.1 2026-07-24). Introspecte via la connection string de stack.md (## Active Database), regroupe les objets en modules, génère 1 User Story par objet SQL et 1 FEAT par module. Multi-dialecte — SQL Server (live-validé), PostgreSQL + Oracle + MySQL/MariaDB (scaffold-validés, runtime live pending — downgrade PostgreSQL audit 2026-08-29). Ne modifie JAMAIS la base. ([zekiriabd/SDD-Pro](https://github.com/zekiriabd/SDD-Pro/blob/HEAD/.sdd/commands/sdd-db-reverse-full.md) · ★196 · 2 copies)

([tribos-dev/wakanda-ia-sdd](https://github.com/tribos-dev/wakanda-ia-sdd/blob/HEAD/sdd-kit/.claude/commands/sdd.build.md))
- **sdd.canary**: Vigila un servicio recién desplegado consultando su health endpoint a intervalos y reporta si deja de responder. Monitoreo ligero post-deploy, sin dependencias externas. ([carlos060798/FORGE](https://github.com/carlos060798/FORGE/blob/HEAD/commands/sdd.canary.md) · ★17)
- **sdd.cancel**: Gracefully cancel a feature in progress, preserving work for potential future resumption. Use when user wants to abandon or pause a feature. ([tribos-dev/wakanda-ia-sdd](https://github.com/tribos-dev/wakanda-ia-sdd/blob/HEAD/sdd-kit/.claude/commands/sdd.cancel.md))
- **sdd.check**: View feature status, progress, and validation results. Use when user wants to check feature health or run validations. ([tribos-dev/wakanda-ia-sdd](https://github.com/tribos-dev/wakanda-ia-sdd/blob/HEAD/sdd-kit/.claude/commands/sdd.check.md))
- **sdd.checklist**: Aplica un checklist de calidad formal a la spec activa — verifica completitud, claridad, testabilidad y alineación con la constitución. "Tests unitarios para los requisitos." ([carlos060798/FORGE](https://github.com/carlos060798/FORGE/blob/HEAD/commands/sdd.checklist.md) · ★17)
- **sdd.compliance**: Genera reporte de cumplimiento regulatorio (GDPR, SOC2, ISO 27001, HIPAA) contra el estado actual del proyecto. ([carlos060798/FORGE](https://github.com/carlos060798/FORGE/blob/HEAD/commands/sdd.compliance.md) · ★17)
- **sdd.comprimir**: Comprime archivos para ahorrar input tokens usando reglas caveman-like adaptadas al español. Optimiza archivos de memoria y config del plugin. ([carlos060798/FORGE](https://github.com/carlos060798/FORGE/blob/HEAD/commands/sdd.comprimir.md) · ★17)
- **sdd.configurar**: Ajusta la configuración de SDD-ES — activar/desactivar agentes, cambiar el modelo asignado a cada uno, modificar comportamientos. ([carlos060798/FORGE](https://github.com/carlos060798/FORGE/blob/HEAD/commands/sdd.configurar.md) · ★17)
- **sdd.constitucion**: Crea o actualiza la constitución del proyecto — el documento fundacional con los principios que guían toda generación posterior. Versionado semántico. ([carlos060798/FORGE](https://github.com/carlos060798/FORGE/blob/HEAD/commands/sdd.constitucion.md) · ★17)
- **sdd.construir**: Pipeline completo de Fragua. Si no hay IR, interpreta primero. Si no hay diseño, diseña. Luego mapea spec → planifica → tareas → implementa. Punto de entrada único para convertir una idea en código. ([carlos060798/FORGE](https://github.com/carlos060798/FORGE/blob/HEAD/commands/sdd.construir.md) · ★17)
- **sdd.crear-agente**: Wizard interactivo para crear un nuevo agente SDD-ES. Genera agents/{nombre}.md, lo registra en plugin.json y actualiza el enrutador de agentes. ([carlos060798/FORGE](https://github.com/carlos060798/FORGE/blob/HEAD/commands/sdd.crear-agente.md) · ★17)
- **sdd.crear-app**: Convierte una descripción en lenguaje natural en una app web o CLI mínima con stack detectado automáticamente, lista para /sdd.desplegar. Orientado a personas con poca experiencia en programación. ([carlos060798/FORGE](https://github.com/carlos060798/FORGE/blob/HEAD/commands/sdd.crear-app.md) · ★17)
- **sdd.crear-mcp**: Convierte una descripción en lenguaje natural en un servidor MCP funcional, empaquetado como .mcpb instalable. Cada Criterio de Aceptación se convierte en una tool MCP. Orientado a no-programadores que quieren publicar capacidades. ([carlos060798/FORGE](https://github.com/carlos060798/FORGE/blob/HEAD/commands/sdd.crear-mcp.md) · ★17)
- **sdd.defect-report**: Generar reporte de calidad con escape rate y mutaciones ([carlos060798/FORGE](https://github.com/carlos060798/FORGE/blob/HEAD/commands/sdd.defect-report.md) · ★17)
- **sdd.descubrir**: Extrae contexto mínimo cuando el usuario describe algo vagamente. Hace preguntas concretas, itera con validaciones y genera el documento de contexto base antes de especificar. ([carlos060798/FORGE](https://github.com/carlos060798/FORGE/blob/HEAD/commands/sdd.descubrir.md) · ★17)
- **sdd.desplegar**: Despliega el producto a su plataforma con verificación previa y health check posterior. Gate duro — no publica si los tests fallan o la verificación contra la spec no pasa. Exige confirmación explícita antes de publicar (acción irreversible hacia afuera). ([carlos060798/FORGE](https://github.com/carlos060798/FORGE/blob/HEAD/commands/sdd.desplegar.md) · ★17)
- **sdd.diseñar**: Fase de diseño de Fragua. Orquesta direction picker → product-designer → architecture-designer → wireframe-mvp → critica-diseno. Requiere IR previo en .sdd/ir.json. ([carlos060798/FORGE](https://github.com/carlos060798/FORGE/blob/HEAD/commands/sdd.dise%C3%B1ar.md) · ★17)
- **sdd.doctor**: Diagnose whether the project's configuration lets the SDD kit do its job. Detects external instructions that contradict, duplicate, or compete with the kit (CLAUDE.md, AGENTS.md, .claude/*, sdd/PROJECT.md, sdd/PATTERNS.md). Use when specs come out bloated, when the agent ignores kit directives, or after major edits to instruction files. ([tribos-dev/wakanda-ia-sdd](https://github.com/tribos-dev/wakanda-ia-sdd/blob/HEAD/sdd-kit/.claude/commands/sdd.doctor.md))
- **sdd.especificar**: Convierte una intención del usuario en una especificación estructurada con criterios de aceptación testeables en formato Dado/Cuando/Entonces. Sin detalles de implementación — solo qué y por qué. ([carlos060798/FORGE](https://github.com/carlos060798/FORGE/blob/HEAD/commands/sdd.especificar.md) · ★17)
- **sdd.estado**: Dashboard del estado actual del flujo SDD — qué fase, qué spec activa, progreso de tareas, próximo paso recomendado. ([carlos060798/FORGE](https://github.com/carlos060798/FORGE/blob/HEAD/commands/sdd.estado.md) · ★17)
- **sdd.exportar**: Exporta el proyecto como bundle portable. Detecta qué agentes/skills/comandos se usaron desde estado.json y genera {nombre}-forge-bundle/ con solo esas piezas + el proyecto + INSTALL.md. ([carlos060798/FORGE](https://github.com/carlos060798/FORGE/blob/HEAD/commands/sdd.exportar.md) · ★17)
- **sdd.finish**: Complete feature implementation, run final validations, and archive. Use when all tasks are done, CI passes, and you're ready to move the feature from wip/ to features/. ([tribos-dev/wakanda-ia-sdd](https://github.com/tribos-dev/wakanda-ia-sdd/blob/HEAD/sdd-kit/.claude/commands/sdd.finish.md))
- **sdd.fix**: Fix validation errors and horizontal consistency issues across spec layers. Use when /sdd.check reports errors, specs are misaligned, or tasks don't match the technical spec. ([tribos-dev/wakanda-ia-sdd](https://github.com/tribos-dev/wakanda-ia-sdd/blob/HEAD/sdd-kit/.claude/commands/sdd.fix.md))
- **sdd.glosario**: Gestiona el glosario del dominio. Añade, edita o consulta términos del dominio del proyecto. ([carlos060798/FORGE](https://github.com/carlos060798/FORGE/blob/HEAD/commands/sdd.glosario.md) · ★17)
- **sdd.go**: Express mode - orchestrates start, spec, plan, build, and finish in one command. Use for rapid feature development when you want the full workflow automated end-to-end with minimal interaction. ([tribos-dev/wakanda-ia-sdd](https://github.com/tribos-dev/wakanda-ia-sdd/blob/HEAD/sdd-kit/.claude/commands/sdd.go.md))
- **sdd.help**: Show help and documentation for SDD Kit commands and workflow. Use when user asks about available commands or how to use the framework. ([tribos-dev/wakanda-ia-sdd](https://github.com/tribos-dev/wakanda-ia-sdd/blob/HEAD/sdd-kit/.claude/commands/sdd.help.md))
- **sdd.hub**: Orchestrate multi-app hub features across apps. Coordinates specs, planning, and build across member apps. Use when working in a hub repo with multiple collaborating apps. ([tribos-dev/wakanda-ia-sdd](https://github.com/tribos-dev/wakanda-ia-sdd/blob/HEAD/sdd-kit/.claude/commands/sdd.hub.md))
- **sdd.implementar**: Ejecuta las tareas con los agentes especializados configurados. Modos: todas en secuencia (sin args), tarea específica (T003), continuar (continuar). Cada tarea se verifica antes de la siguiente. ([carlos060798/FORGE](https://github.com/carlos060798/FORGE/blob/HEAD/commands/sdd.implementar.md) · ★17)
- **sdd.import**: Import external specifications or existing code into the SDD workflow. Use when user has existing specs or wants to onboard existing code. ([tribos-dev/wakanda-ia-sdd](https://github.com/tribos-dev/wakanda-ia-sdd/blob/HEAD/sdd-kit/.claude/commands/sdd.import.md))
- **sdd.importar**: Importa una especificación externa (Markdown, issue de GitHub/GitLab, doc compartido) y la convierte al formato SDD-ES. ([carlos060798/FORGE](https://github.com/carlos060798/FORGE/blob/HEAD/commands/sdd.importar.md) · ★17)
- **sdd.interpretar**: Punto de entrada de Fragua. Convierte una idea en texto libre a un IR JSON validado. Orquesta discovery + interpreter + validación. Primer comando para cualquier proyecto nuevo. ([carlos060798/FORGE](https://github.com/carlos060798/FORGE/blob/HEAD/commands/sdd.interpretar.md) · ★17)
- **sdd.list**: List all features in the meli workspace with their status and phase. Use when user wants to see current features. ([tribos-dev/wakanda-ia-sdd](https://github.com/tribos-dev/wakanda-ia-sdd/blob/HEAD/sdd-kit/.claude/commands/sdd.list.md))
- **sdd.mapear**: Indexa la estructura del proyecto, símbolos públicos y dependencias. Genera mapas estáticos en .sdd/mapa/ para que Claude consulte en vez de escanear archivos. ([carlos060798/FORGE](https://github.com/carlos060798/FORGE/blob/HEAD/commands/sdd.mapear.md) · ★17)
- **sdd.modo**: Cambia o muestra el modo de trabajo de la sesión (normal/rapido/prototipo). Modifica sesion.modo en sdd.config.yaml para que todos los comandos lo lean automáticamente. ([carlos060798/FORGE](https://github.com/carlos060798/FORGE/blob/HEAD/commands/sdd.modo.md) · ★17)
- **sdd.optimizar**: Alias de /sdd.comprimir — ciclo de ahorro de tokens (fan-out, modelo por fase, compresión de memoria, auditoría de caché y presupuesto). Úsalo cuando el contexto o el gasto se disparen. ([carlos060798/FORGE](https://github.com/carlos060798/FORGE/blob/HEAD/commands/sdd.optimizar.md) · ★17)
- **sdd.optimizar-memoria**: Alias de /sdd.comprimir — comprime y deduplica la memoria de agentes (.sdd/memoria/agente-*.md) cuando supera 50 KB. ([carlos060798/FORGE](https://github.com/carlos060798/FORGE/blob/HEAD/commands/sdd.optimizar-memoria.md) · ★17)
- **sdd.plan**: Create an implementation plan from a feature specification. ([mikecubed/clean-code-codex](https://github.com/mikecubed/clean-code-codex/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sdd.plan.md))
- **sdd.plan**: Generate implementation tasks from approved specifications. Use when both functional and technical specs are approved and user is ready to break down work into executable tasks with effort estimates. ([tribos-dev/wakanda-ia-sdd](https://github.com/tribos-dev/wakanda-ia-sdd/blob/HEAD/sdd-kit/.claude/commands/sdd.plan.md))
- **sdd.planificar**: Genera el plan técnico de implementación desde la spec — arquitectura, contratos de API, modelo de datos, archivos afectados, riesgos. Delega a los agentes especializados configurados. ([carlos060798/FORGE](https://github.com/carlos060798/FORGE/blob/HEAD/commands/sdd.planificar.md) · ★17)
- **sdd.project**: Initialize or manage PROJECT.md configuration file. Use when user needs to set up project conventions or edit project settings. ([tribos-dev/wakanda-ia-sdd](https://github.com/tribos-dev/wakanda-ia-sdd/blob/HEAD/sdd-kit/.claude/commands/sdd.project.md))
- **sdd.qa**: Prueba el producto en un navegador real generando casos E2E a partir de los Criterios de Aceptación de la spec activa. Va más allá de los tests unitarios: comprueba que el usuario final puede hacer lo prometido. Usa un MCP de navegador (Playwright). ([carlos060798/FORGE](https://github.com/carlos060798/FORGE/blob/HEAD/commands/sdd.qa.md) · ★17)
- **sdd.release**: Genera una nueva versión semántica del proyecto. Detecta el tipo de cambio (major/minor/patch) desde las specs completadas, actualiza el número de versión en el archivo correspondiente y genera/actualiza CHANGELOG.md. ([carlos060798/FORGE](https://github.com/carlos060798/FORGE/blob/HEAD/commands/sdd.release.md) · ★17)
- **sdd.retro**: Retrospectiva ligera tras completar una feature o despliegue. Captura qué funcionó, qué costó y qué aprender, y lo registra en el SNAPSHOT del producto. Cierra el ciclo del sprint. ([carlos060798/FORGE](https://github.com/carlos060798/FORGE/blob/HEAD/commands/sdd.retro.md) · ★17)
- **sdd.reverse-eng**: Reverse engineer existing codebase to generate SDD specifications. Use when user wants to create specs from existing code. ([tribos-dev/wakanda-ia-sdd](https://github.com/tribos-dev/wakanda-ia-sdd/blob/HEAD/sdd-kit/.claude/commands/sdd.reverse-eng.md))
- **sdd.rollback**: Rollback feature to a previous workflow phase, preserving git history. Use when user needs to redo a phase. ([tribos-dev/wakanda-ia-sdd](https://github.com/tribos-dev/wakanda-ia-sdd/blob/HEAD/sdd-kit/.claude/commands/sdd.rollback.md))
- **sdd.snapshot**: Actualiza el SNAPSHOT.md del producto con las funcionalidades activas. Se ejecuta después de completar una spec. ([carlos060798/FORGE](https://github.com/carlos060798/FORGE/blob/HEAD/commands/sdd.snapshot.md) · ★17)
- **sdd.spec**: Create and approve functional and technical specifications. Use when user needs to define requirements (functional) or design architecture (technical). Supports --approve, --iterate, --summary, and --audio flags. ([tribos-dev/wakanda-ia-sdd](https://github.com/tribos-dev/wakanda-ia-sdd/blob/HEAD/sdd-kit/.claude/commands/sdd.spec.md))
- **sdd.specify**: Create a feature specification from a natural language description. ([mikecubed/clean-code-codex](https://github.com/mikecubed/clean-code-codex/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sdd.specify.md))
- **sdd.start**: Initialize new feature in SDD Kit framework. Use when user wants to begin a new feature, set up the sdd/wip/ directory structure, and configure project metadata. Supports --reopen for archived features. ([tribos-dev/wakanda-ia-sdd](https://github.com/tribos-dev/wakanda-ia-sdd/blob/HEAD/sdd-kit/.claude/commands/sdd.start.md))
- **sdd.tareas**: Desglosa el plan en tareas atómicas, ordenadas por dependencias, con criterios de verificación concretos y asignación de agente responsable. ([carlos060798/FORGE](https://github.com/carlos060798/FORGE/blob/HEAD/commands/sdd.tareas.md) · ★17)
- **sdd.tasks**: Generate an actionable, dependency-ordered task list from the feature plan. ([mikecubed/clean-code-codex](https://github.com/mikecubed/clean-code-codex/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sdd.tasks.md))
- **sdd.verificar**: Verifica que el código implementado cumple TODOS los criterios de aceptación de la spec original. Auditoría independiente con el agente revisor. ([carlos060798/FORGE](https://github.com/carlos060798/FORGE/blob/HEAD/commands/sdd.verificar.md) · ★17)
- **sdd:add-task**: creates draft task file in .specs/tasks/draft/ with original user intent ([dalawwa/labor-methods](https://github.com/dalawwa/labor-methods/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sdd-add-task.md))
- **sdd:brainstorm**: Use when creating or developing, before writing code or implementation plans - refines rough ideas into fully-formed designs through collaborative questioning, alternative exploration, and incremental validation. - **sdk-api.availability**: 1. Load the symbol index for `--module` (extract + cache on first use). 2. Find every symbol whose qualified name matches exactly, or — failing an exact match — whose qualified name or title matches case-insensitively. All matches are returned (a symbol can have several declarations). 3. Project each match to `SymbolOut` and emit. The macOS availability lives in each match's `availability` field. ([markmals/apple-platform-tools](https://github.com/markmals/apple-platform-tools/blob/HEAD/Features/sdk-api/0001-symbol-queries/commands/sdk-api.availability.md))
- **sdk-api.check**: 1. Load the symbol index for `--module` (extract + cache on first use). 2. Look up `&lt;symbol&gt;` by exact qualified name. When several declarations share the name (overloads, cross-extension redeclarations), the **first** match is reported — this is an existence check, not an enumeration. 3. Project the matched symbol (if any) to the `SymbolOut` shape and emit. ([markmals/apple-platform-tools](https://github.com/markmals/apple-platform-tools/blob/HEAD/Features/sdk-api/0001-symbol-queries/commands/sdk-api.check.md))
- **sdk-api.enums**: 1. Load the symbol index for `--module` (extract + cache on first use). 2. Collect the members of `&lt;type&gt;` and keep only those whose kind is an enum case (`swift.enum.case`). 3. Project each case to `SymbolOut` and emit. A type with no enum cases yields an empty `cases` array, not an error. ([markmals/apple-platform-tools](https://github.com/markmals/apple-platform-tools/blob/HEAD/Features/sdk-api/0001-symbol-queries/commands/sdk-api.enums.md))
- **sdk-api.members**: 1. Load the symbol index for `--module` (extract + cache on first use). 2. Resolve `&lt;type&gt;` to its container symbol and collect every symbol related to it by `memberOf`, sorted by member title. 3. Project each member to `SymbolOut` and emit. An unknown type yields an empty `members` array, not an error. ([markmals/apple-platform-tools](https://github.com/markmals/apple-platform-tools/blob/HEAD/Features/sdk-api/0001-symbol-queries/commands/sdk-api.members.md))
- **sdk-api.search**: 1. Load the symbol index for `--module` (extract + cache on first use). 2. Rank symbols against the lowercased query by a fixed precedence — exact title &gt; title prefix &gt; qualified-name prefix &gt; title contains &gt; qualified-name contains — breaking ties by title, and take the first `--limit`. 3. Project each result to `SymbolOut` and emit. ([markmals/apple-platform-tools](https://github.com/markmals/apple-platform-tools/blob/HEAD/Features/sdk-api/0001-symbol-queries/commands/sdk-api.search.md))

- **sdk-search.get**: 1. Load the embedded corpus. 2. For each id in order, look it up. Found patterns are projected to `PatternOutput`; unknown ids are collected as missing. 3. Emit the found patterns: a **single object** when exactly one id was found, otherwise a **JSON array** of pattern objects. 4. If any id was missing, write a notice to stderr and exit 1 — the found patterns are still printed to stdout first. ([markmals/apple-platform-tools](https://github.com/markmals/apple-platform-tools/blob/HEAD/Features/sdk-search/0001-pattern-search/commands/sdk-search.get.md))
- **sdk-search.list**: 1. Load the embedded corpus. 2. Walk the patterns in corpus order, grouping by `category`. With `--category`, keep only patterns whose category matches case-insensitively. 3. Emit groups in the order each category first appears in the corpus; within a group, patterns keep corpus order. ([markmals/apple-platform-tools](https://github.com/markmals/apple-platform-tools/blob/HEAD/Features/sdk-search/0001-pattern-search/commands/sdk-search.list.md))
- **sdk-search.search**: 1. Load the embedded corpus and build the BM25 search engine. 2. Reject an empty query (no positional args) with a `ValidationError`. 3. **Single-query mode** (exactly one positional arg, including a single quoted multi-word string): run the search and emit a `SearchOutput`. 4. **Batch mode** (two or more positional args, each a distinct query): run each query in order and emit a `BatchSearchOutpu ([markmals/apple-platform-tools](https://github.com/markmals/apple-platform-tools/blob/HEAD/Features/sdk-search/0001-pattern-search/commands/sdk-search.search.md))

- **sdlc-auto**: Full SDLC automation for a Jira story. Quality-assesses and triages if needed, then delegates the complexity decision to the shared triage protocol (refs/triage.md, applied inline) and routes a `full` story through the two-phase spec → review-gate → plan+impl-single-PR flow, or a `lightweight` story (&lt;= threshold pts, inclusive) straight to implementation. Posts Jira comments at each gate with clickable PR links. Pass --async-review for non-blocking service-driven execution where JSON-RPC events replace the human confirmation gate. ([whimzyLive/nightshift-ai](https://github.com/whimzyLive/nightshift-ai/blob/HEAD/docs/reference/commands/sdlc-auto.md) · ★3)

- **sdp-be-router**: Use when you need to expose REST endpoints for a resource, wire HTTP methods to handlers, or group routes under a path prefix in a backend ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sdp-be-router.md) · 2 copies)
- **sdp-be-service**: Use when domain business logic spans multiple entities or repositories, when rules or transactions are leaking into a handler or use case, or when persistence and HTTP concerns are mixed into one class ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.cursor/commands/sdp-be-service.md) · 2 copies)
- **sdp-be-usecase**: Use when a backend operation spans services/repos, handler logic grows fat, or a transaction needs one owner ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sdp-be-usecase.md) · 2 copies)
- **sdp-checklist**: Use when a feature spec exists and needs requirement-quality audit before implementation. ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sdp-checklist.md) · 2 copies)
- **sdp-clarify**: Use when a feature spec has vague adjectives, open decisions or TODO markers and you need to resolve them before planning, or when acceptance criteria are not yet testable. ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.cursor/commands/sdp-clarify.md) · 2 copies)
- **sdp-constitution**: Use when creating, amending, ratifying, or versioning the project constitution, or when project principles drift from the inherited organizational law. ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sdp-constitution.md) · 2 copies)
- **sdp-debug**: Use when a test fails, an error appears during implementation, or a bug resists the first fix attempt ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sdp-debug.md) · 2 copies)
- **sdp-fe-design-to-react**: Use when you have a design screenshot, Figma/Sketch/XD export, or wireframe and need the matching React component built ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.cursor/commands/sdp-fe-design-to-react.md) · 2 copies)
- **sdp-fe-expo-api-route**: Use when adding a server-side endpoint inside an Expo Router app, when a BFF route is missing validation or auth, or when business logic leaks into an API route handler ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sdp-fe-expo-api-route.md) · 2 copies)
- **sdp-fe-expo-native-module**: Use when you need to access a device API or native platform capability that the Expo SDK does not cover, or when a JS-only implementation is too slow and the work must run in Swift/Kotlin. - **sdp-fe-expo-screen**: Use when adding a route under app/ in an Expo Router app, when a screen renders blank while loading, when a data error is swallowed silently, or when logic leaks into app/ ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sdp-fe-expo-screen.md) · 2 copies)
- **sdp-fe-filter**: Use when you need a filter bar that lets users search and narrow down a list or data table by one or more fields ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sdp-fe-filter.md) · 2 copies)
- **sdp-fe-form**: Use when a screen needs to collect user input for an entity. ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.cursor/commands/sdp-fe-form.md) · 2 copies)
- **sdp-fe-page**: Use when you need to scaffold a new routed view for a feature ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sdp-fe-page.md) · 2 copies)
- **sdp-fe-service**: Use when a component is calling fetch or axios directly, when API URLs and request shapes are scattered across pages, or when you need a typed client layer for an API resource ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.cursor/commands/sdp-fe-service.md) · 2 copies)
